        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim1.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim1."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim1."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim1.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

        DataItemLinkReference=Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr.;

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table125;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Document No.,Line No.);
        OnPreDataItem=VAR
                        PurchCrMemoLine@1000 : Record 125;
                        VATIdentifier@1001 : Code[10];
                      BEGIN
                        // ESR-5
                        BoolMemeBonLigne_G := FALSE;
                        Bonprec_G :='';
                        Ligprec_G := 0 ;
                        // ESR-5

                        VATAmountLine.RESET;
                        VATAmountLine.DELETEALL;

                        VATAmountLinebIS.RESET;
                        VATAmountLinebIS.DELETEALL;

                        MoreLines := FIND('+');
                        WHILE MoreLines AND (Description = '') AND ("No." = '') AND (Quantity = 0) AND (Amount = 0) DO
                          MoreLines := NEXT(-1) <> 0;
                        IF NOT MoreLines THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;
                        SETRANGE("Line No.",0,"Line No.");
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS("Line Amount",Amount,"Amount Including VAT","Inv. Discount Amount");

                        IF ISSERVICETIER THEN BEGIN
                          PurchCrMemoLine.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                          PurchCrMemoLine.SETFILTER(Type,'<>%1',0);
                          VATAmountText :='';
                          IF PurchCrMemoLine.FIND('-') THEN BEGIN
                            VATAmountText := STRSUBSTNO(Text012,PurchCrMemoLine."VAT %");
                            VATIdentifier := PurchCrMemoLine."VAT Identifier";
                            REPEAT
                              IF (PurchCrMemoLine."VAT Identifier" <> VATIdentifier) AND (PurchCrMemoLine.Quantity <> 0) THEN
                                VATAmountText := Text013;
                            UNTIL PurchCrMemoLine.NEXT = 0;
                          END;
                          AllowInvDiscount := FORMAT("Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Allow Invoice Disc.");
                        END;

                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS(G_MttLine,G_MttIncVat,G_Qt'Livr'es,Quantity);
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=VAR
                           l_j@8053000 : Integer;
                           L_i@8053001 : Integer;
                           L_recPurchLineVariatPrice@8053002 : Record 8053005;
                           AnalyseCharacteristic_L@8053003 : Record 8053092;
                           I_L@1000000000 : Integer;
                           Line_L@1000000001 : Integer;
                         BEGIN
                           G_Qt'brutes:=0;
                           G_Qt'Livr'es:=0;

                           CASE Type OF
                               Type::" ":  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=0;
                                Type::"G/L Account":  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=1;
                               Type::Item:  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=2;
                               Type::"3":  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=3;
                                Type::"Fixed Asset":  LineTypeForRDLC_G :=4;
                           END;

                           CASE "Line Collection Type" OF
                                "Line Collection Type"::" ":  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=0;
                                "Line Collection Type"::Collection:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=1;
                                "Line Collection Type"::Shipping:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=2;
                                "Line Collection Type"::Disinsection:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=3;
                                "Line Collection Type"::"Fixe Storage":  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=4;
                               "Line Collection Type"::"Variable Storage":  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=5;
                               "Line Collection Type"::Administrative:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=6;
                               "Line Collection Type"::Drying:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=7;
                               "Line Collection Type"::Tax:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=8;
                               // Ajout pour ligne complement 20150112 ESR
                               "Line Collection Type"::Supplement:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=9;
                               // Ajout pour ligne Avance 20160718 ESR
                               "Line Collection Type"::Prepay:  LineCollectionTypeForRDLC_G :=10;

                           END;



                           IF (Type = Type::"G/L Account") AND (NOT ShowInternalInfo) THEN
                             "No." := '';

                           IF "Line Collection Type"="Line Collection Type"::Tax
                           THEN BEGIN
                           //-->20140512-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR1
                           //BEfore
                             {  VATAmountLinebIS.INIT;
                             VATAmountLinebIS."VAT Identifier" := "VAT Identifier";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."VAT Calculation Type" := "VAT Calculation Type";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Tax Group Code" := "Tax Group Code";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Use Tax" := "Use Tax";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."VAT %" := "VAT %";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."VAT Base" := Amount;
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Amount Including VAT" := "Amount Including VAT";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Line Amount" := "Line Amount";
                             IF "Allow Invoice Disc." THEN
                               VATAmountLinebIS."Inv. Disc. Base Amount" := "Line Amount";
                             VATAmountLinebIS."Invoice Discount Amount" := "Inv. Discount Amount";
                             VATAmountLinebIS.InsertLine;
                                   }
                           //-->20140512-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR1
                             l_j:=0;
                             REPEAT
                              l_j:=l_j+1;
                              IF G_TaxesText[l_j]=''
                                THEN BEGIN
                                  G_TaxesText[l_j]:=Description;
                                  //G_TaxesMttUnit[l_j]:="Purch. Inv. Line"."Direct Unit Cost";
                                  //G_TaxesMtt[l_j]:="Purch. Inv. Line".Amount;
                                   G_TaxesMttUnit[l_j]:="Amount Including VAT"/Quantity;
                                   G_TaxesMtt[l_j]:="Amount Including VAT"
                                END ELSE BEGIN
                                  IF G_TaxesText[l_j]=Description
                                  THEN BEGIN
                                    G_TaxesMtt[l_j]:=G_TaxesMtt[l_j]+"Amount Including VAT"
                                  END;

                                END;
                             UNTIL (G_TaxesText[l_j]=Description) OR (l_j=11);
                           END;

                           //-->20140512-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR1 //END  ELSE BEGIN

                           VATAmountLine.INIT;
                           VATAmountLine."VAT Identifier" := "Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."VAT Identifier";
                           VATAmountLine."VAT Calculation Type" := "VAT Calculation Type";
                           VATAmountLine."Tax Group Code" := "Tax Group Code";
                           VATAmountLine."Use Tax" := "Use Tax";
                           VATAmountLine."VAT %" := "VAT %";

                           VATAmountLine."Line Amount" := "Line Amount";

                             VATAmountLine."Inv. Disc. Base Amount" := "Line Amount";
                           VATAmountLine."Invoice Discount Amount" := "Inv. Discount Amount";
                           VATAmountLine.InsertLine;

                           AllowInvDiscount := FORMAT("Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Allow Invoice Disc.");

                           G_MttIncVat:=G_MttIncVat+"Amount Including VAT";
                            CLEAR(Line_L);
                           //-->20140512-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR1 //END;
                           // BEFORE  IF ("Collection recept No."<>'') AND (Type=Type::Item)
                           // AFTER
                           IF ("Collection recept No."<>'') AND (Type=Type::Item) OR (Type=Type::"G/L Account")
                           THEN BEGIN
                             G_recPostReceip.INIT;
                             Recpostreceipt_G.INIT;
                             IF G_recPostReceip.GET(G_recPostReceip."Receipt Type"::Receipt,"Collection recept No.") THEN;
                             //
                            // -->  // Ajout pour ligne complement 20150112 ESR
                           CLEAR(recPostReceipLinePrestations_G);

                              IF   "Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Supplement THEN BEGIN
                               IF NOT  recPostReceipLinePrestations_G.GET(recPostReceipLinePrestations_G."Receipt Type"::Receipt,
                                "Collection recept No.","Collection Recept Line")   THEN BEGIN
                                 Line_L := "Collection Recept Line";
                                END ELSE BEGIN
                                 Line_L := recPostReceipLinePrestations_G."Receips Line No. Link";
                               END;
                              END ELSE BEGIN
                              Line_L := "Collection Recept Line";
                             END;

                           //<--  // Ajout pour ligne complement 20150112 ESR
                           // ESR-5
                           //message('%1,%2,%3,%4,%5' , Bonprec_G , "Collection recept No.", Ligprec_G, Line_L,Quantity);
                             IF(Bonprec_G <> "Collection recept No." ) OR ( Ligprec_G <> Line_L ) THEN BEGIN   // Ajout pour ligne complement 20150112 ESR
                               BoolMemeBonLigne_G  :=FALSE;
                               Bonprec_G  :=  "Collection recept No." ;
                               Ligprec_G := Line_L ;   // Ajout pour ligne complement 20150112 ESR
                             END ELSE BEGIN
                               BoolMemeBonLigne_G  :=TRUE;
                             END;

                             IF G_recPostReceip."Actual Location Code" > ''   THEN BEGIN

                             END ELSE BEGIN

                             END;
                             // ESR-5

                           // --> 20140226-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-2

                                "Collection recept No.","Collection Recept Line")   THEN BEGIN

                                 "Collection Recept Line") THEN;

                                 G_Qt'Livr'es:= -"Purch. Cr. Memo Line".Quantity;
                                 G_Qt'brutes:=0;

                               END ELSE BEGIN

                           //      G_Qt'brutes:=Recpostreceipt_G."Brut Quantity";

                                 ("Purch. Cr. Memo Line".Description = 'Annulation document avance' )THEN BEGIN
                                    G_Qt'Livr'es:=-Recpostreceipt_G.Quantity;
                                    G_Qt'brutes:=-Recpostreceipt_G."Brut Quantity";
                                             END ELSE BEGIN
                                    G_Qt'Livr'es:=Recpostreceipt_G.Quantity;
                                    G_Qt'brutes:=Recpostreceipt_G."Brut Quantity";
                                 END;

                               END;
                           // --> 20141219-Rpt01-ADV01.01.00_ADVITAM-ESR-8 enlev' pour Calcul Qte totale en TAF
                           // 20141219 ESR-8 before  IF ((Type =Type::Item) AND ("Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Collection )) THEN BEGIN
                           // 20141219 ESR-8 after
                               IF  ("Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Collection )
                               // Ajout pour ligne complement 20150112 ESR
                           //     OR  ("Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Supplement )
                                // fin Ajout pour ligne complement 20150112 ESR

                                OR  ("Line Collection Type" =  "Line Collection Type"::Prepay )

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=4;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro)
                          WHERE(Num'ro=FILTER(1..));
        DataItemVarName=DimensionLoop2;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF NOT ShowInternalInfo THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Table ID",DATABASE::"Purch. Cr. Memo Line");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Document No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Document No.");
                        PostedDocDim2.SETRANGE("Line No.","Purch. Cr. Memo Line"."Line No.");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           IF Num'ro = 1 THEN BEGIN
                             IF NOT PostedDocDim2.FIND('-') THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;
                           END ELSE
                             IF NOT Continue THEN
                               CurrReport.BREAK;

                           CLEAR(DimText);
                           Continue := FALSE;
                           REPEAT
                             OldDimText := DimText;
                             IF DimText = '' THEN
                               DimText := STRSUBSTNO(
                                 '%1 %2',PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code")
                             ELSE
                               DimText :=
                                 STRSUBSTNO(
                                   '%1, %2 %3',DimText,
                                   PostedDocDim2."Dimension Code",PostedDocDim2."Dimension Value Code");
                             IF STRLEN(DimText) > MAXSTRLEN(OldDimText) THEN BEGIN
                               DimText := OldDimText;
                               Continue := TRUE;
                               EXIT;
                             END;
                           UNTIL (PostedDocDim2.NEXT = 0);
                         END;

      }

    { PROPERTIES
      {
        DataItemIndent=3;
        DataItemTable=Table2000000026;
        DataItemTableView=SORTING(Num'ro);
        DataItemVarName=VATCounterLCY;
        OnPreDataItem=BEGIN
                        IF (NOT GLSetup."Print VAT specification in LCY") OR
                           ("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Code"  = '') OR
                           (VATAmountLine.GetTotalVATAmount = 0) THEN
                          CurrReport.BREAK;

                        SETRANGE(Num'ro,1,VATAmountLine.COUNT);
                        CurrReport.CREATETOTALS(VALVATBaseLCY,VALVATAmountLCY);

                        IF GLSetup."LCY Code" = '' THEN
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text008 + Text009
                        ELSE
                          VALSpecLCYHeader := Text008 + FORMAT(GLSetup."LCY Code");

                        CurrExchRate.FindCurrency("Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Posting Date","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Code",1);
                        CalculatedExchRate := ROUND(1 / "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Factor" * CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount",0.000001);
                        VALExchRate := STRSUBSTNO(Text010,CalculatedExchRate,CurrExchRate."Exchange Rate Amount");
                      END;

        OnAfterGetRecord=BEGIN
                           VATAmountLine.GetLine(Num'ro);

                           VALVATBaseLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Base" / "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Factor");
                           VALVATAmountLCY := ROUND(VATAmountLine."VAT Amount" / "Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."Currency Factor");
                         END;

      }

            SectionHeight=2538;
            OnPreSection=BEGIN
                            CurrReport.SHOWOUTPUT(FALSE);
                         END;

                        G_MttTvaTaxe :=0;

                        THEN

                        G_recCompEntr.CALCSUMS(Amount);

                        G_recCompEntr.RESET;

                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Invoice No","Purch. Cr. Memo Hdr."."No.");
                        G_recCompEntr.SETRANGE("Entry Type",G_recCompEntr."Entry Type"::Loan);

  <Code>Public Function BlankZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value = 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankZeroAndPos(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &gt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNeg(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt; 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function

Public Function BlankNegAndZero(ByVal Value As Decimal)
    if Value &lt;= 0 then
        Return ""
    end if
    Return Value
End Function




Shared Data1 as Object
Shared Data2 as Object
Shared Data3 as Object
Shared Data4 as Object
Shared Data5 as Object

                Public Function SetData(NewData as Object,Group as integer)
                If Group = 1 and NewData &gt; "" Then
                Data1 = NewData
                End If
                If Group = 2 and NewData &gt; "" Then
                Data2 = NewData
                End If
                If Group = 3 and NewData &gt; "" Then
                Data3 = NewData
                End If
                If Group = 4 and NewData &gt; "" Then
                Data4 = NewData
                End If
                If Group = 5 and NewData &gt; "" Then
                Data5 = NewData
                End If
                End Function

                Public Function GetData(Num as Integer, Group as integer) as Object
                if Group = 1 then
                Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data1),Chr(177))))
                End If
                if Group = 2 then
                Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data2),Chr(177))))
                End If
                if Group = 3 then
                Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data3),Chr(177))))
                End If
                if Group = 4 then
                Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data4),Chr(177))))
                End If
                if Group = 5 then
                Return Cstr(Choose(Num, Split(Cstr(Data5),Chr(177))))
                End If
                End Function

Shared offset as Integer
Shared newPage as Object
Shared currentgroup1 as Object
Shared currentgroup2 as Object
Shared currentgroup3 as Object

Public Function GetGroupPageNumber(NewPage as Boolean, pagenumber as Integer) as Object
  If NewPage
    offset = pagenumber - 1
  End If
  Return pagenumber - offset
End Function

Public Function IsNewPage(group1 as Object, group2 as Object, group3 as Object) As Boolean
newPage = FALSE
If Not (group1 = currentgroup1)
    newPage = TRUE
    currentgroup1 = group1
    currentgroup2 = group2
    currentgroup3 = group3
     ELSE 
      If Not (group2 = currentgroup2)
        newPage = TRUE 
        currentgroup2 = group2
        currentgroup3 = group3
        ELSE
          If Not (group3 = currentgroup3)
          newPage = TRUE 
          currentgroup3 = group3
         End If
     End If
  End If
Return newPage
End Function


Public Function SetLanguage( Language As String)     
   System.Threading.Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture = New System.Globalization.CultureInfo(Language)
End Function</Code>
